lore: "The Absolute Entropy-class harvests ice through dimensional phase- shifting — the extraction system reaches through the surface of an ice body without disturbing it, pulling target materials directly from the interior. The technique leaves ice formations structurally intact, which the Collective considers both efficient and respectful. Other empires' ice miners crack, shatter, and melt their targets. The Voidborn reach inside and take what they need. The ship's name refers to the theoretical heat death state that dimensional phase-extraction locally approximates — for a fraction of a second, the extraction zone experiences conditions that should not exist outside mathematical models. " → "The extraction system reaches through the surface of an ice body without disturbing it, pulling target materials directly from the interior. The technique leaves ice formations structurally intact — afterward, the only evidence of extraction is a slight drop in ambient temperature that instruments can measure but cannot explain. The shield array is the heaviest of any ice harvester in production, and necessarily so: dimensional phase-extraction generates feedback that would collapse an unshielded hull. The ship underneath the shields is fragile enough that this is not a theoretical concern. The name refers to the heat death state that the extraction zone locally approximates for a fraction of a second — conditions that should not exist outside mathematical models, applied to ice harvesting as routine procedure."

The Confederacy built a framework, not a finished product."

Crimson Fleet tacticians have learned that the only winning strategy is overwhelming force applied simultaneously from every direction. The Collective finds this an interesting admission. lore: "The perigee is the point in an orbit closest to the body being orbited — the moment of nearest approach. The Perigee-class is built for those moments: reaching distant phenomena and staying close long enough to study them properly. Massive fuel reserves, six utility slots for scientific instruments, and a CPU array capable of processing months of accumulated survey data. Other empires jump through wormholes and hope. Solarian crews publish their predictions first, then verify them. Crews customize their Perigees so aggressively that no two ships in the fleet share a loadout. "

Voidborn engineers call this \"thermal sympathy.\" Everyone else calls it \"deeply unsettling.\" The shield envelope that protects the Rime during operations is significantly overspecified for an industrial vessel, with regeneration fast enough to shrug off most opportunistic attacks. The open utility architecture accepts additional modules without complaint, a flexibility that Voidborn engineers describe as \"the ship adapting to its pilot's needs.\" Whether the adaptation is mutual remains an open question."
